) and using triangle inequality, we get the first part of (
4.4
) by taking
\delta
sufficiently small depending on
n,s,L
and
\epsilon
.

Step 4
:
Uniform bound on
|D^{\tau}d_{s}v|
. From Lemma
4.2
along with (
4.1
), we observe that

\displaystyle\|D^{\tau}d_{s}v\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathcal{Q}_{1})}=\sup_{(x,y,t)\in\mathcal{Q}_{1}}\frac{|v(x,t)-v(y,t)|}{|x-y|^{s+\tau}}\leq c\left(\frac{1}{\tau}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}v|^{2}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+c\Tail_{\infty,2s}\left(v-(v)_{B_{2}}(t);Q_{2}\right),

where
c=c(n,s,L,\tau)
. Proceeding as in Step 2 and
[
13
, Lemma 3.3]
, it can be shown that the right-hand side quantity of the above expression is bounded by a uniform constant
c=c(n,s,L,\tau)
. This completes the proof of the lemma.
∎

We finish this section by giving a non-scaled version of the above lemma and this directly follows from Lemma
4.3
along with a scaling argument (see Lemma
1.2
).

Lemma 4.4
.

Let
Q_{20\rho_{i}}(z_{i})\Subset\Omega_{T}
. For any
\epsilon>0
, there is a constant
\delta=\delta(n,s,L,\epsilon)
such that for any weak solution
u
to (
1.1
) satisfying

\frac{1}{\tau}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{20\rho_{i}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{2}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}+\Tail_{\infty,2s}\left(\frac{u-(u)_{B_{20\rho_{i}(x_{i})}}(t)}{(20\rho_{i})^{s+\tau}}\,;\,Q_{20\rho_{i}}(z_{i})\right)^{2}\leq 1

and

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{\tau}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{20\rho_{i}}(z_{i})}\left((20\rho_{i})^{s-\tau}|G|\right)^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{2}{\gamma}}+\frac{1}{\tau}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{20\rho_{i}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{2}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}+\Tail_{2,s}\left(\frac{f-(f)_{B_{20\rho_{i}}(x_{i})}(t)}{(20\rho_{i})^{\tau}}\,;\,Q_{20\rho_{i}}(z_{i})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{10\rho_{i}}(z_{i})}|A-(A)_{10\rho_{i},x_{i}}(t)|\,dx\,dy\,dt\right)^{2}\leq\delta^{2},

then there exists a solution
v
to

v_{t}+\mathcal{L}^{\Phi}_{A_{{10\rho_{i},x_{i}}}(t)}v=0\quad\text{in }Q_{10\rho_{i}}(z_{i})

such that

(4.11)

\frac{1}{\tau}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{5\rho_{i}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}(u-v)|^{2}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\leq\epsilon^{2}\quad\text{and}\quad\left\|D^{\tau}d_{s}v\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{5\rho_{i}}(z_{i})\right)}\leq c,

where
c=c(n,s,L,\tau)
.

5.
Coverings of upper level sets

In this section, we construct parabolic cylinders covering the upper level set of
d_{s}u
, where

u\in L^{2}\left(\Lambda_{2}\,;\,W^{s,2}(B_{2})\right)\cap C\left(\Lambda_{2};L^{2}(B_{2})\right)\cap L^{\infty}\left(\Lambda_{2}\,;\,L^{1}_{2s}(\mathbb{R}^{n})\right)

is a weak solution to the localized problem:

(5.1)

u_{t}+\mathcal{L}_{A}^{\Phi}u=(-\Delta)^{\frac{s}{2}}f+g\quad\text{in }Q_{2}.

In addition, we assume that
f\in L^{q}\left(\Lambda_{2};L^{1}_{s}\left(\mathbb{R}^{n}\right)\right)
and

\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}+|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{q}+|G|^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}<\infty,

where
p\in[2,q]
,

(5.2)

\tau\in\left(0,s-\frac{2s}{q}\right)

and
the constant
\gamma
is defined in (
3.2
).
Let us denote

(5.3)

\tilde{q}=\frac{1}{2}\left(q+\frac{2s}{s-\tau}\right)

to see that

(5.4)

s>\tau+\frac{2s}{\tilde{q}}

and

(5.5)

\tilde{q}<q,

which follow from the choice of
\tau
given in (
5.2
).
We point out that (
5.4
) and (
5.5
) are needed to handle the tail induced by the right-hand side
f
and to employ Fubini’s theorem, respectively (see (
5.27
) and (
6.10
)).
We now present the main proposition of this section.

Proposition 5.1
.

Let
1\leq r_{1}<r_{2}\leq 2
,
\delta>0
and
u
be a weak solution to (
5.1
). Then, there are two families of countable disjoint cylinders
\left\{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})\right\}_{i\geq 0}
and
\left\{\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{{j}}}\left(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j}\right)\right\}_{j\geq 0}
such that

(5.6)

U_{\lambda}:=\left\{(x,y,t)\in\mathcal{Q}_{r_{1}}\,:\,|D^{\tau}d_{s}u(x,y,t)|\geq\lambda\right\}\subset\left(\bigcup\limits_{i\geq 0}\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{2}{s}}\rho_{{i}}}\left(z_{i}\right)\right)\bigcup\left(\bigcup\limits_{j\geq 0}\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{{j}}}\left(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j}\right)\right)

whenever
\lambda\geq\lambda_{0}
, where

(5.7)

\displaystyle\lambda_{0}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\frac{c\tau^{\frac{1}{q}-\frac{1}{\gamma}}}{(r_{2}-r_{1})^{\frac{5n}{s}}}\left(\left(\frac{1}{\tau}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\Tail_{\infty,2s}\left(\frac{u-(u)_{B_{2}}(t)}{2^{s+\tau}};Q_{2}\right)+\left(\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{2}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{B_{2}}\frac{|u-(u)_{Q_{2}}|^{2}}{2^{2s+2\tau}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{c\tau^{\frac{1}{q}-\frac{1}{\gamma}}}{(r_{2}-r_{1})^{\frac{5n}{s}}}\frac{1}{\delta}\left(\left(\frac{1}{\tau}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2}}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{q}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}+\Tail_{q,s}\left(\frac{f-(f)_{B_{2}}(t)}{2^{\tau}};Q_{2}\right)+\left(\frac{1}{\tau}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2}}(2^{s-\tau}|G|)^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\right)

for some constant
c=c(n,s,L,q,\tau)
. In particular, there exist constants
a_{u}=a_{u}(n,s,L,q,\tau)\in(0,1]
,
a_{f}=a_{f}(n,s,L,q,\tau)\in(0,1]
and
a_{g}=a_{g}(n,s,L,q,\tau)\in(0,1]
such that

(5.8)

\displaystyle\sum_{i\geq 0}\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})\right)+\sum_{j\geq 0}\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{{j}}}\left(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c}{\lambda^{p}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{r_{2}}\cap\{|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|>a_{u}\lambda\}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}+\frac{c}{(\delta\lambda)^{\tilde{q}}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{r_{2}}\cap\{|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|>a_{f}\delta\lambda\}}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}

\displaystyle+\frac{c}{(\delta\lambda)^{b_{\tau}}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{r_{2}}\cap\{|G|^{\gamma}>(a_{g}\delta\lambda)^{b_{\tau}}G_{0}^{-1}\}}|G|^{\gamma}G_{0}\,d\mu_{\tau,t},

where we denote

(5.9)

b_{\tau}=\frac{2n+4s}{n+2s+2\tau}\quad\text{and}\quad G_{0}=\left(\int_{Q_{2}}|g|^{\gamma}\,dz\right)^{\frac{b_{\tau}-\gamma}{\gamma}}.

In addition, we get that

(5.10)

\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{{j}}}\left(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j}\right)}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p_{\#}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{{p_{\#}}}}\leq c_{od}\lambda\quad\text{for any }j

and for some constant
c_{od}=c_{od}(n,s,p)
, where the constant
p_{\#}
is defined in (
2.1
),

Remark 5
.

As we pointed out earlier, (
5.2
) is only employed to control the tail term of
f
. Thus if
f=0
, we can remove the condition (
5.2
).

Proof.

We first define the functional

(5.11)

\displaystyle\Theta_{D}\left(z_{0},r\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{r}(z_{0})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\tau^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\left(\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{r}(t_{0})}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{B_{r}(x_{0})}\frac{|u-(u)_{Q_{r}(z_{0})}|^{2}}{r^{2s+2\tau}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\delta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{r}(z_{0})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\tilde{q}}}+\frac{1}{\delta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{r}(z_{0})}(r^{s-\tau}|G|)^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}

for any
z_{0}\in Q_{r_{1}}
and
r>0
with
Q_{r}(z_{0})\subset Q_{2}
.
The rest of the proof is divided into 8 steps.

Step 1. Coverings for the diagonal part.

Let us take

(5.12)

\displaystyle\lambda_{0}

\displaystyle=\frac{M\kappa^{-1}\tau^{\frac{1}{q}}}{(r_{2}-r_{1})^{\frac{5n}{s}}}\left(\left(\frac{1}{\tau}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\Tail_{\infty,2s}\left(\frac{u-(u)_{B_{2}}(t)}{2^{s+\tau}};Q_{2}\right)+\left(\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{2}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{B_{2}}\frac{|u-(u)_{Q_{2}}|^{2}}{2^{2s+2\tau}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{M\kappa^{-1}\tau^{\frac{1}{q}}}{(r_{2}-r_{1})^{\frac{5n}{s}}}\frac{1}{\delta}\left(\left(\frac{1}{\tau}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2}}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{q}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}+\Tail_{q,s}\left(\frac{f-(f)_{B_{2}}(t)}{2^{\tau}};Q_{2}\right)+\left(\frac{1}{\tau}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2}}(2^{s-\tau}|G|)^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\right),

where
M\geq 1
and
\kappa\in(0,1]
are free parameters which we will determine later (see (
5.18
) and (
5.36
)). More precisely, the parameter
M
will be used to handle the diagonal part and the parameter
\kappa
will be used to handle the non-diagonal part. We next take a positive integer
j_{0}\geq 5
such that

(5.13)

\frac{16(c_{0}+\tilde{c}+2c_{q})^{2}}{1-2^{-s+\tau+\frac{2s}{\tilde{q}}}}\leq 2^{j_{0}\left(s-\tau-\frac{2s}{\tilde{q}}\right)},

where
c_{0}
is the constant determined in Lemma
3.3
, and
\tilde{c}
and
c_{q}
are the constants determined in (
2.6
). Using (
5.3
), we observe that the number
j_{0}
depends only on
n,s,L,q
and
\tilde{q}
.
We then note that for any
z_{0}\in Q_{r_{1}}
,

Q_{5^{\frac{2}{s}}\times 2^{j_{0}+3}\mathcal{R}_{1,2}}(z_{0})\subset Q_{r_{2}},

where we denote

(5.14)

\mathcal{R}_{1,2}=2^{-j_{0}-3}\times 5^{-\frac{2}{s}}\times(s(r_{2}-r_{1}))^{\frac{1}{s}}.

Let us now define for
\lambda\geq\lambda_{0}
,

D_{\kappa\lambda}=\left\{z_{0}\in Q_{r_{1}}\,:\,\sup_{0<\rho\leq\mathcal{R}_{1,2}}\Theta_{D}\left(z_{0},\rho\right)>\kappa\lambda\right\}.

Since
\tau<1
, we observe that for any
z_{0}\in Q_{r_{1}}
and
r\in\left[\mathcal{R}_{1,2},5^{\frac{2}{s}}\times 2^{j_{0}+3}\mathcal{R}_{1,2}\right]
,

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{r}(z_{0})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\frac{1}{\delta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{r}(z_{0})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\tilde{q}}}+\frac{1}{\delta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{r}(z_{0})}(r^{s-\tau}|G|)^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\leq\frac{\kappa\lambda}{4}

holds by assuming

(5.15)

M\geq 2^{10n(j_{0}+4+5s^{-1})}s^{-\frac{5n}{s}}.

In addition, using Lemma
3.3
and the fact that
\tau<1
with
\gamma<2
, we have

(5.16)

\displaystyle\tau^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\left(\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{r}(t_{0})}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{B_{r}(x_{0})}\frac{|u-(u)_{Q_{r}(z_{0})}|^{2}}{r^{2s+2\tau}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq c_{0}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2r}(z_{0})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{2}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+c_{0}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2r}(z_{0})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{2}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}+c_{0}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2r}(z_{0})}\left((2r)^{s-\tau}|G|\right)^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{c_{0}\tau^{\frac{1}{2}}\Tail_{2,2s}\left(\frac{u-(u)_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}(t)}{(2r)^{s+\tau}};Q_{2r}(z_{0})\right)}_{T_{1}}+\underbrace{c_{0}\tau^{\frac{1}{2}}\Tail_{2,s}\left(\frac{f-(f)_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}(t)}{(2r)^{\tau}};Q_{2r}(z_{0})\right)}_{T_{2}},

where the constant
c_{0}
is determined in Lemma
3.3
.
By Hölder’s inequality and (
2.6
), we further estimate
T_{1}
and
T_{2}
as

(5.17)

\displaystyle T_{1}+T_{2}

\displaystyle\leq c_{0}\tau^{\frac{1}{2}}\Tail_{p,2s}\left(\frac{|u-(u)_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}(t)|}{(2r)^{s+\tau}};Q_{2r}(z_{0})\right)+c_{0}\tau^{\frac{1}{2}}\Tail_{\tilde{q},s}\left(\frac{|f-(f)_{B_{2r}(x_{0})}(t)|}{(2r)^{\tau}};Q_{2r}(z_{0})\right)

\displaystyle\leq c_{0}c_{p}\left(\frac{2}{\mathcal{R}_{1,2}}\right)^{s+\tau+\frac{2s}{p}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+c_{0}c_{q}\left(\frac{2}{\mathcal{R}_{1,2}}\right)^{\tau+\frac{2s}{q}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2}}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{q}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}

\displaystyle+c_{0}\tilde{c}\left(\frac{2}{\mathcal{R}_{1,2}}\right)^{5n}\Tail_{p,2s}\left(\frac{u-(u)_{B_{2}}(t)}{2^{s+\tau}};Q_{4}\right)+c_{0}\tilde{c}\left(\frac{2}{\mathcal{R}_{1,2}}\right)^{5n}\Tail_{q,s}\left(\frac{f-(f)_{B_{2}}(t)}{2^{\tau}};Q_{2}\right),

where the constants
c_{p},c_{q}
and
\tilde{c}
are determined in Lemma
2.3
.
Using Remark
4
and Hölder’s inequality to the third term on the right-hand side of (
5.17
),
we deduce from (
5.16
) that

\tau^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\left(\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{r}(t_{0})}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{B_{r}(x_{0})}\frac{|u-(u)_{Q_{r}(z_{0})}|^{2}}{r^{2s+2\tau}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\frac{\kappa\lambda}{4}

holds by taking

(5.18)

M=(c_{0}c_{q}+c_{0}\tilde{c})2^{10n(j_{0}+4+5s^{-1})}s^{-\frac{5n}{s}}

which clearly satisfies (
5.15
).
As a result, we observe that for any
z_{0}\in Q_{r_{1}}
and
r\in\left[\mathcal{R}_{1,2},5^{\frac{2}{s}}\times 2^{j_{0}+3}\mathcal{R}_{1,2}\right]
,

(5.19)

\Theta_{D}(z_{0},r)\leq\kappa\lambda.

Therefore, for each
z\in D_{\kappa\lambda}
, there is an exit radius
\rho_{z}\leq\mathcal{R}_{1,2}
such that

(5.20)

\Theta_{D}\left(z,\rho_{z}\right)\geq\kappa\lambda\quad\text{and}\quad\Theta_{D}\left(z,\rho\right)\leq\kappa\lambda\quad\text{ if }\rho_{z}\leq\rho\leq 5^{\frac{2}{s}}\times 2^{j_{0}+3}\mathcal{R}_{1,2}.

We first observe that if
Q_{\rho}(z_{1})\cap Q_{r}(z_{2})\neq\emptyset
with
\frac{\rho}{2}\leq r\leq 2\rho
, then we have
Q_{r}(z_{2})\subset Q_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho}(z_{1})
.
Thus we apply Vitali’s covering lemma to the collection
\{Q_{2^{j_{0}}\rho_{z}}(z)\}_{z\in D_{\kappa\lambda}}
, in order to
find a family of mutually disjoint countable cylinders

(5.21)

\left\{Q_{2^{j_{0}}\rho_{z_{i}}}(z_{i})\right\}_{i\geq 0}\text{ such that }D_{\kappa\lambda}\subset\bigcup_{z_{0}\in D_{\kappa\lambda}}Q_{\rho_{z_{0}}}(z_{0})\subset\bigcup\limits_{i=0}^{\infty}Q_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\times 2^{j_{0}}\rho_{z_{i}}}(z_{i}).

In addition, by the proof of Vitali’s covering lemma, we get that for any
z\in D_{\kappa\lambda}
, there is
i
such that

(5.22)

\frac{2^{j_{0}}\rho_{z_{i}}}{2}\leq 2^{j_{0}}\rho_{z}\leq 2^{j_{0}+1}\rho_{z_{i}}\quad\text{and}\quad Q_{2^{j_{0}}\rho_{z}}(z)\subset Q_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\times\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i}),

where we denote

(5.23)

\rho_{i}=2^{j_{0}}\rho_{z_{i}}\quad\text{for each }i.

From (
5.20
), we have

(5.24)

\displaystyle\kappa\lambda

\displaystyle\leq\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{z_{i}}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\tau^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\left(\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{\rho_{z_{i}}}(t_{i})}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{B_{\rho_{z_{i}}}(x_{i})}\frac{|u-(u)_{Q_{\rho_{z_{i}}}(z_{i})}|^{2}}{\rho_{z_{i}}^{2s+2\tau}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\delta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{z_{i}}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\tilde{q}}}+\frac{1}{\delta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{z_{i}}}(z_{i})}\left((\rho_{z_{i}})^{s-\tau}|G|\right)^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}.

We first note from (
2.6
) with
\beta=s
,
\tilde{s}=s
and
k=j_{0}
that

(5.25)

\displaystyle\tau^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\Tail_{p,2s}\left(\frac{u-(u)_{B_{2\rho}(x_{i})}(t)}{(2\rho)^{s+\tau}};Q_{2\rho}(z_{i})\right)

\displaystyle\leq c_{p}\sum_{j=2}^{j_{0}}2^{i\left(-s+\tau+\frac{2s}{p}\right)}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2^{j}\rho}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle+\tilde{c}\tau^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\sum_{j=j_{0}+1}^{l}2^{j(-s+\tau)}\left(\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{2^{j}\rho}(t_{i})}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{B_{2^{j}\rho}(x_{i})}\frac{|u-(u)_{B_{2^{j}\rho}(x_{i})}(t)|^{2}}{(2^{j}\rho)^{2{s}+2\tau}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\tilde{c}\tau^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}2^{-2sl}\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)^{s+\tau}\left(\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{2}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{B_{2}}\frac{|u-(u)_{B_{2}}(t)|^{2}}{2^{2s+2\tau}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\tilde{c}}{(2-r_{1})^{n+2s}}\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)^{-s+\tau}\Tail_{\infty,2s}\left(\frac{u-(u)_{B_{2}}(t)}{2^{\tilde{s}+\tau}};Q_{2}\right),

where
\rho=\rho_{z_{i}}
and
l
is the positive integer such that
2^{j_{0}+1}\mathcal{R}_{1,2}\leq 2^{l}\rho_{z_{i}}<2^{j_{0}+2}\mathcal{R}_{1,2}
.
We point out that for the last tail term in (
5.25
), we have taken supremum in the time variable for the expression in (
2.7
).
Similarly, we observe

(5.26)

\displaystyle\tau^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\Tail_{\tilde{q},s}\left(\frac{f-(f)_{B_{2\rho}(x_{i})}(t)}{(2\rho)^{\tau}};Q_{2\rho}(z_{i})\right)

\displaystyle\leq c_{\tilde{q}}\sum_{j=2}^{j_{0}}2^{i\left(-s+\tau+\frac{2s}{\tilde{q}}\right)}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2^{j}\rho}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\tilde{q}}}+c_{\tilde{q}}\sum_{j=j_{0}+1}^{l}2^{i\left(-s+\tau+\frac{2s}{\tilde{q}}\right)}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2^{j}\rho}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\tilde{q}}}

\displaystyle+c_{\tilde{q}}2^{-sl}\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)^{\tau+\frac{2s}{\tilde{q}}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2}}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{q}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}+\frac{\tilde{c}}{(2-r_{1})^{n+2s}}\left(\frac{2}{\rho}\right)^{-s+\tau+\frac{2s}{q}}\Tail_{q,s}\left(\frac{f-(f)_{B_{2}}(t)}{2^{\tau}};Q_{2}\right),

where we have used Hölder’s inequality for the third and fourth terms in the right-hand side of (
5.26
). We combine (
5.16
) with
r=\rho_{z_{i}}
, (
5.25
) and (
5.26
) together with (
5.4
), (
5.23
) and Remark
4
, in order to get

(5.27)

\displaystyle\tau^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{\rho_{z_{i}}}(t_{i})}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{B_{\rho_{z_{i}}}(x_{i})}\frac{|u-(u)_{Q_{\rho_{z_{i}}}(z_{i})}|^{2}}{\rho_{z_{i}}^{2s+2\tau}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{i}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{i}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\tilde{q}}}+c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})}\left((\rho_{{i}})^{s-\tau}|G|\right)^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}

\displaystyle+c_{0}\tilde{c}\left[\sum_{j=j_{0}+1}^{l}2^{i\left(-s+\tau\right)}\Theta_{D}\left(z_{j},2^{j}\rho_{z_{i}}\right)+\tau^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\left(\frac{2}{2^{j_{0}}\mathcal{R}_{1,2}}\right)^{s+\tau}\left(\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{2}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{B_{2}}\frac{|u-(u)_{B_{2}}(t)|^{2}}{2^{2s+2\tau}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]

\displaystyle+c_{0}c_{q}\left[\sum_{j=j_{0}+1}^{l}2^{i\left(-s+\tau+\frac{2s}{\tilde{q}}\right)}\Theta_{D}\left(z_{j},2^{j}\rho_{z_{i}}\right)+\left(\frac{2}{2^{j_{0}}\mathcal{R}_{1,2}}\right)^{\tau+\frac{2s}{\tilde{q}}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2}}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{q}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{q}}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{c_{0}\tilde{c}}{(r_{2}-r_{1})^{5n}}\left(\Tail_{\infty,2s}\left(\frac{u-(u)_{B_{2}}(t)}{2^{s+\tau}};Q_{2}\right)+\Tail_{q,s}\left(\frac{f-(f)_{B_{2}}(t)}{2^{\tau}};Q_{2}\right)\right),

where
c=c(n,s,L,q,\tau)
. We here highlight that (
5.4
) is necessary to handle the sixth term in the right-hand side of (
5.27
), as
\sum\limits_{j=j_{0}+1}^{\infty}2^{i\left(-s+\tau+\frac{2s}{\tilde{q}}\right)}<\frac{2^{j_{0}\left(-s+\tau+\frac{2s}{\tilde{q}}\right)}}{1-2^{-s+\tau+\frac{2s}{\tilde{q}}}}
.
We further estimate the right-hand side of (
5.27
) using (
5.4
), (
5.13
), (
5.14
), (
5.18
) and (
5.20
) as

\displaystyle\tau^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{\rho_{z_{i}}}(t_{i})}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{B_{\rho_{z_{i}}}(x_{i})}\frac{|u-(u)_{Q_{\rho_{z_{i}}}(z_{i})}|^{2}}{\rho_{z_{i}}^{2s+2\tau}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{i}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{i}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\tilde{q}}}+c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})}\left((\rho_{{i}})^{s-\tau}|G|\right)^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}+\frac{\kappa\lambda}{4}.

Plugging the above estimate into (
5.24
) along with (
5.23
), we find that

\kappa\lambda\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{i}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\frac{c}{\delta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{i}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\tilde{q}}}+\frac{c}{\delta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})}\left((\rho_{{i}})^{s-\tau}|G|\right)^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}

for some constant
c=c(n,s,L,q,\tau)
.
Therefore we deduce that one of the following must hold:

(5.28)

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa\lambda}{3}\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{i}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}},\quad\frac{\kappa\lambda}{3}\leq\frac{c}{\delta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{i}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\tilde{q}}},

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa\lambda}{3}\leq\frac{c}{\delta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})}\left((\rho_{{i}})^{s-\tau}|G|\right)^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}.

If the first inequality or the second inequality in (
5.28
) holds, then we get

(5.29)

\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})\right)\leq\frac{c}{(\kappa\lambda)^{p}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\quad\text{or}\quad\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})\right)\leq\frac{c}{(\kappa\delta\lambda)^{\tilde{q}}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}.

On the other hand, if the third inequality in (
5.28
) holds, we observe that

\displaystyle\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})\right)\leq\frac{c}{(\kappa\delta\lambda)^{\gamma}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})}\left((\rho_{{i}})^{s-\tau}|G|\right)^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}=\frac{c}{(\kappa\delta\lambda)^{\gamma}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})}\left((\rho_{{i}})^{s-\tau}|G|\right)^{\gamma}G_{0}G_{0}^{-1}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}.

We note from (
5.9
) that

\displaystyle\rho_{i}^{\gamma(s-\tau)}G_{0}^{-1}\leq\rho_{i}^{\gamma(s-\tau)}\left(\int_{{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})}|g|^{\gamma}\right)^{\frac{\gamma-b_{\tau}}{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c\rho_{i}^{\gamma(s-\tau)}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})}\rho_{i}^{n+2s}|G|^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{\gamma-b_{\tau}}{\gamma}}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})}\left((\rho_{{i}})^{s-\tau}|G|\right)^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{\gamma-b_{\tau}}{\gamma}}.

Using the above two estimates along with the third inequality in (
5.28
), we have

(5.30)

\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})\right)\leq\frac{c}{(\delta\kappa\lambda)^{b_{\tau}}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})}|G|^{\gamma}G_{0}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}.

We combine (
5.29
) and (
5.30
) to see that

\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})\right)\leq\frac{c}{(\kappa\lambda)^{p}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}+\frac{c}{(\kappa\delta\lambda)^{\tilde{q}}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}+\frac{c}{(\delta\kappa\lambda)^{b_{\tau}}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{{i}}}(z_{i})}|G|^{\gamma}G_{0}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}.

A suitable choice of the constants
\tilde{a}_{u}=\tilde{a}_{u}(n,s,L,q,\tau)\in\left(0,\frac{1}{8}\right]
,
\tilde{a}_{f}=\tilde{a}_{f}(n,s,L,q,\tau)\in(0,1]
and
\tilde{a}_{g}=\tilde{a}_{g}(n,s,L,q,\tau)\in(0,1]
yields

(5.31)

\displaystyle\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{i}}(z_{i})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c}{(\kappa\lambda)^{p}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{i}}(z_{i})\cap\{|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|>\tilde{a}_{u}\kappa\lambda\}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}+\frac{c}{(\kappa\delta\lambda)^{\tilde{q}}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{i}}(z_{i})\cap\{|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|>\tilde{a}_{f}\kappa\delta\lambda\}}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}

\displaystyle+\frac{c}{(\kappa\delta\lambda)^{b_{\tau}}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{i}}(z_{i})\cap\{|G|^{\gamma}>(\tilde{a}_{g}\kappa\delta\lambda)^{b_{\tau}}G_{0}^{-1}\}}|G|^{\gamma}G_{0}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}.

Remark 6
.

We here remark on the second term appearing on the right-hand side of (
5.11
). We first note that this term is used to handle parabolic tail terms.
Since
u\in C\left(-2^{2s},2^{2s};L^{2}(B_{2})\right)
, there may exist some points
z_{0}\in Q_{r_{1}}
such that
\Theta_{D}(z_{0},\rho_{z_{0}})\geq\kappa\lambda
and

\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{z_{0}}}(z_{0})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\leq\frac{\kappa\lambda}{2}\quad\text{with}\quad\tau^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\left(\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{\rho_{z_{0}}}(t_{0})}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{B_{\rho_{z_{0}}}(x_{0})}\frac{|u-(u)_{Q_{\rho_{z_{0}}}(z_{0})}|^{2}}{\rho_{z_{0}}^{2s+2\tau}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\geq\frac{\kappa\lambda}{2},

where
\rho_{z_{0}}
is the exit-radius of the point
z_{0}
.
However, from energy estimates and rigorous tail estimates, we still have a sufficiently good bound on the measure of such cylinders as in (
5.31
), which is an essential ingredient to obtain
L^{q}
-regularity of
D^{\tau}d_{s}u
.

Step 2. Coverings for off-diagonal parts.
We first note that for any
(x,y,t)\in\mathcal{Q}_{r_{1}}
and
r\in\left(0,5^{\frac{2}{s}}\times 2^{j_{0}+3}{\mathcal{R}_{1,2}}\right]
,
we have

\mathcal{Q}_{r}(x,y,t)\subset\mathcal{Q}_{r_{2}},

where the parameter
\mathcal{R}_{1,2}
is defined in (
5.14
).
Let us define

(5.32)

\displaystyle E_{p,\tau}\left(u\,;\,Q_{r}(z_{0})\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{r}(z_{0})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\tau^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\left(\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{r}(t_{0})}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{B_{r}(x_{0})}\frac{|u-(u)_{Q_{r}(z_{0})}|^{2}}{r^{2s+2\tau}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\delta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{r}(z_{0})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\tilde{q}}},

for any
Q_{r}(z_{0})\Subset Q_{2}
. Then we first note that for any
\rho<r
,

(5.33)

\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{\rho}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{B_{\rho}}\frac{|u-(u)_{Q_{\rho}}|^{2}}{\rho^{2s+2\tau}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq 2\left(\frac{r}{\rho}\right)^{\frac{n}{2}+s+\tau}\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{r}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{B_{r}}\frac{|u-(u)_{Q_{r}}|^{2}}{r^{2s+2\tau}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

We now intend to employ exit-time arguments, to this end, we first introduce a set function which is defined by

(5.34)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{A}\left(\mathcal{B}_{r}\right)=\begin{cases}\left(\frac{r}{\mathrm{dist}\left({B^{1}_{r}},{B^{2}_{r}}\right)}\right)^{s+\tau}&\text{if }\mathrm{dist}\left(B_{r}^{1},B_{r}^{2}\right)\geq r,\\
1&\text{if }\mathrm{dist}\left(B_{r}^{1},B_{r}^{2}\right)<r,\end{cases}

where
\mathcal{B}_{r}=B_{r}^{1}\times B_{r}^{2}
. We next define a functional

(5.35)

\displaystyle\Theta_{OD}\left(u;\mathcal{Q}_{r}(x_{1},x_{2},t_{0})\right)

\displaystyle=\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{r}(x_{1},x_{2},t_{0})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+{\mathfrak{A}\left(\mathcal{B}_{r}(x_{1},x_{2})\right)}\sum_{d=1}^{2}E_{p,\tau}\left(u\,;\,Q_{r}(x_{d},t_{0})\right).

We then observe from (
2.4
) and (
5.19
) that for any
r\in\left[5^{-\frac{2}{s}}{\mathcal{R}_{1,2}},5^{\frac{2}{s}}\times 2^{j_{0}+3}\mathcal{R}_{1,2}\right]
,

\Theta_{OD}\left(u;\mathcal{Q}_{r}(x_{1},x_{2},t_{0})\right)\leq\lambda

holds by taking

(5.36)

\kappa=\frac{\tau^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}}{2^{4nj_{0}}5^{\frac{20n}{s}}(C_{n}+1)},

where the constants
C_{n}
and
j_{0}
are determined in (
2.4
) and (
5.13
), respectively.
Let us define

OD_{\lambda}=\left\{(x_{1},x_{2},t_{0})\in\mathcal{Q}_{r_{1}}\,:\,\sup_{0<\rho\leq 5^{-\frac{2}{s}}{\mathcal{R}_{1,2}}}\Theta_{OD}\left(u;\mathcal{Q}_{\rho}(x_{1},x_{2},t_{0})\right)\geq\lambda\right\}.

For each
(x_{1},x_{2},t_{0})\in OD_{\lambda}
, there is an exit-time radius
\overline{r}\leq 5^{-\frac{2}{s}}{\mathcal{R}_{1,2}}
such that

(5.37)

\Theta_{OD}\left(u;\mathcal{Q}_{\rho}(x_{1},x_{2},t_{0})\right)\leq\lambda\quad\text{if }\rho\geq\overline{r}\quad\text{and}\quad\Theta_{OD}\left(u;\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}}(x_{1},x_{2},t_{0})\right)\geq\lambda.

Using Vitali’s covering lemma, we find a collection
\widetilde{\mathcal{A}}=\left\{\mathcal{Q}_{2^{j_{0}}\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\right\}_{j\geq 0}
whose elements are mutually disjoint and satisfy

OD_{\lambda}\subset\bigcup_{j\geq 0}\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}2^{j_{0}}\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j}),

where we denote by
\overline{r}_{j}
the exit-time radius of the point
(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})
.
Therefore, we have

(5.38)

\left\{(x,y,t)\in\mathcal{Q}_{r_{1}}\,:\,|D^{\tau}d_{s}u(x,y,t)|\geq\lambda\right\}\subset\bigcup_{j\geq 0}\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j}),

where we denote

(5.39)

\displaystyle\widetilde{r}_{j}\equiv 2^{j_{0}}\overline{r}_{j}.

Remark 7
.

We give some remarks on the functional defined in (
5.35
). We first note that the second term in the right-hand side of (
5.35
) is needed to obtain a bound on the
L^{p_{\#}}
-norm of
D^{\tau}d_{s}u
(see (
5.49
), below). We now explain how to obtain a good upper bound on the measure of
\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\in\widetilde{\mathcal{A}}
which is an essential ingredient to get
L^{q}
-regularity of
D^{\tau}d_{s}u
. Indeed, by (
2.5
), (
5.13
) and (
5.39
), we observe

\displaystyle\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\right)\leq c\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\right)

for some constant
c=c(n,s,\Lambda,q,\tau)
, which implies that
it suffices to investigate a good upper bound on the measure of
\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})
.
Suppose that the selected cylinder
\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})
is close to the diagonal. In the next step, we will see that such a cylinder is indeed contained in a diagonal cylinder which we did choose in step 1. Therefore, the measure of
\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})
has a good upper bound as in (
5.31
).

On the other hand, if the selected cylinder
\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\in{\widetilde{\mathcal{A}}}
is not close to the diagonal, then
from (
5.37
), we observe that there holds

(5.40)

\displaystyle\frac{3\lambda}{4}\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\right)}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\cap\left\{|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|>\frac{\lambda}{16}\right\}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle+\mathfrak{A}\left(\mathcal{B}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j})\right)^{s+\tau}\left[\sum_{d=1}^{2}E_{p,\tau}\left(u\,;\,Q_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{d,j},t_{0,j})\right)\right].

Our approach to obtaining a good upper bound on the measure of such cylinders depends on the size of
E_{p,\tau}\left(u\,;\,Q_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{d,j},t_{0,j})\right)
. Indeed, if
E_{p,\tau}\left(u\,;\,Q_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{d,j},t_{0,j})\right)<\frac{\lambda}{16}
, then the second term on the right-hand side of (
5.40
) can be absorbed to the left-hand side. Thus we have a good upper bound on the measure of
\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\in\widetilde{\mathcal{A}}
(see (
5.65
) in step 6 below). We next suppose
E_{p,\tau}\left(u\,;\,Q_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{d,j},t_{0,j})\right)\geq\frac{\lambda}{16}
. Then we first note that if

(5.41)

\lambda<a+b+c\Longrightarrow\mbox{$\left(\frac{\lambda}{3}\right)^{p}<a^{p}$, $\left(\frac{\lambda}{3}\right)^{\widetilde{q}}<b^{\widetilde{q}}$ or $\left(\frac{\lambda}{3}\right)^{\gamma}<c^{\gamma}$},

where
a,b
and
c
are nonnegative constants. Applying this simple observation to (
5.40
), we get (
5.71
). Thus it remains to obtain a suitable upper bound on the second term in the right-hand side of (
5.71
). To this end, we find a suitable diagonal cylinder which was chosen in step 1 and contains
\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{d,j},t_{0,j})
, and then we use some combinatorial arguments by taking advantage of the factor
\mathfrak{A}\left(\mathcal{B}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j})\right)
(see step 7 for more details). As a result, we obtain a good upper bound on the measure of
\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\in\widetilde{\mathcal{A}}
.

Step 3. First elimination of off-diagonal cylinders.

We now prove that if
\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\in\widetilde{\mathcal{A}}
satisfies

(5.42)

\mathrm{dist}\left(B_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j}),B_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{2,j})\right)<5^{\frac{1}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j},

then

(5.43)

\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\subset\bigcup\limits_{i}\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{{i}}}\left(z_{i}\right).

By (
5.37
), one of the followings must hold:

(5.44)

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}>\frac{\lambda}{3},\hskip 9.24994ptE_{p,\tau}\left(u\,;\,{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,i},t_{0,i})\right)>\frac{\lambda}{3},

\displaystyle E_{p,\tau}\left(u\,;\,{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{2,i},t_{0,i})\right)>\frac{\lambda}{3}.

Suppose that the first inequality in (
5.44
) holds. We now observe

\mathcal{B}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j})\subset\mathcal{B}_{5^{\frac{2}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j}).

Therefore, using (
2.4
), (
5.12
) and (
5.36
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{2}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,j})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\geq\left(\frac{\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\right)}{\mu_{\tau,t}\left({\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{2}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,j})}\right)}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\geq\kappa\lambda,

which implies that
\Theta_{D}\left((x_{1,j},t_{0,j}),5^{\frac{2}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}\right)>\kappa\lambda
. By the fact that
5^{\frac{2}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}\leq\mathcal{R}_{1,2}
and (
5.20
), (
5.21
) yields

\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{2}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,j})\subset\bigcup\limits_{i}\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{{i}}}\left(z_{i}\right).

We next assume that the second inequality in (
5.44
) is true. By (
5.36
), we have
\Theta_{D}\left((x_{1,j},t_{0,j}),5^{\frac{2}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}\right)>\kappa\lambda
. Since
\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\subset\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{2}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,j})
which follows by (
5.42
), we have

\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\subset\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{2}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,j})\subset\bigcup\limits_{i\geq 0}\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{{i}}}\left(z_{i}\right).

Similarly, we get (
5.43
) if the third inequality in (
5.44
) holds.
Thus, we now focus on the following subfamily of
\widetilde{\mathcal{A}}
:

(5.45)

\mathcal{A}=\left\{\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\ \middle|\begin{array}[]{l}\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\in\widetilde{\mathcal{A}}\text{ and }\\
\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\not\subset\bigcup\limits_{i\geq 0}\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{2}{s}}\rho_{{i}}}\left(z_{i}\right)\text{ for each }i\end{array}\right\}

Indeed, we take cylinders
\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{2}{s}}\rho_{{i}}}\left(z_{i}\right)
instead of the cylinder
\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{{i}}}\left(z_{i}\right)
, in order to eliminate other types of nearly diagonal cylinders (see (
5.53
) below).

Step 4. Off-diagonal estimates.
We now obtain a bound on
L^{p_{\#}}
-norm of
D^{\tau}d_{s}u
and a reverse Hölder’s inequality on cylinders which are far from the diagonal.

Lemma 5.2
.

Let
\mathcal{Q}=\mathcal{Q}_{r}(x_{1},x_{2},t_{0})\subset\mathcal{Q}_{2}
be such that

(5.46)

\mathrm{dist}(B_{r}(x_{1}),B_{r}(x_{2}))\geq r.

Then we have

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p_{\#}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p_{\#}}}

\displaystyle\leq c\,\Theta_{OD}\left(u;\mathcal{Q}\right)

for some constant
c=c(n,s,p,\tau)
.

Proof.

We observe from (
5.46
) that

(5.47)

{\dist\left(B_{r}(x_{1}),B_{r}(x_{2})\right)}\leq|x-y|\leq 5\dist\left(B_{r}(x_{1}),B_{r}(x_{2})\right)

whenever
x\in B_{r}(x_{1})
and
y\in B_{r}(x_{2})
. The above inequality and Jensen’s inequality yield that

(5.48)

\frac{{r}^{2n}}{c(n)\dist\left(B_{r}(x_{1}),B_{r}(x_{2})\right)^{n-2\tau}}\leq\mu_{\tau}\left(\mathcal{B}_{r}(x_{1},x_{2})\right)\leq{\frac{c(n){r}^{2n}}{\dist\left(B_{r}(x_{1}),B_{r}(x_{2})\right)^{n-2\tau}}}

and

\displaystyle\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{{r}}(x_{1},x_{2},t_{0})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p_{\#}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c}{\dist\left(B_{r}(x_{1}),B_{r}(x_{2})\right)^{p_{\#}\left(s+\tau\right)}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{Q_{{r}}(x_{1},t_{0})}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{B_{r}(x_{1})}|u(x,t)-u(y,t)|^{p_{\#}}\,dz\,dt

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c{r}^{p_{\#}(s+\tau)}}{\dist\left(B_{r}(x_{1}),B_{r}(x_{2})\right)^{p_{\#}\left(s+\tau\right)}}\sum_{d=1}^{2}\underbrace{\frac{1}{{r}^{p_{\#}(s+\tau)}}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{Q_{{r}}(x_{d},t_{0})}|u(x,t)-(u)_{B_{{r}}(x_{d})}(t)|^{p_{\#}}\,dz}_{=I_{d}}

\displaystyle+\frac{c{r}^{p_{\#}(s+\tau)}}{\dist\left(B_{r}(x_{1}),B_{r}(x_{2})\right)^{p_{\#}\left(s+\tau\right)}}\underbrace{\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\Lambda_{{r}}(t_{0})}\frac{|(u)_{B_{r}(x_{1})}(t)-(u)_{B_{r}(x_{2})}(t)|^{p_{\#}}}{{r}^{p_{\#}(s+\tau)}}\,dt}_{=J}

for some constant
c=c(n,s,p)
. We now further estimate
I_{1}
,
I_{2}
and
J
as below.

Estimates of
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
.

Using (
5.46
) and Lemma
2.1
, we estimate
I_{d}
as

\displaystyle I_{d}

\displaystyle\leq c\left(\frac{1}{\tau}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{{r}}(x_{d},t_{0})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)\left(\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{{r}}(t_{0})}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{B_{{r}}(x_{d})}\frac{\left|u-(u)_{Q_{{r}}(x_{d},t_{0})}\right|^{2}}{{r}^{2s+2\tau}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{sp}{n}},

where we have used the fact that

\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{{r}}(t_{0})}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{B_{{r}}(x_{d})}\frac{\left|u-(u)_{B_{{r}}(x_{d})}(t)\right|^{2}}{{r}^{2s+2\tau}}\,dx\leq\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{{r}}(t_{0})}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{B_{{r}}(x_{d})}\frac{\left|u-(u)_{Q_{{r}}(x_{d},t_{0})}\right|^{2}}{{r}^{2s+2\tau}}\,dx.

Applying Young’s inequality to the above inequality, we have

\frac{{r}^{p_{\#}(s+\tau)}}{\dist\left(B_{r}(x_{1}),B_{r}(x_{2})\right)^{p_{\#}\left(s+\tau\right)}}\sum_{d=1}^{2}I_{d}\leq\frac{c{r}^{p_{\#}(s+\tau)}}{\dist\left(B_{r}(x_{1}),B_{r}(x_{2})\right)^{p_{\#}\left(s+\tau\right)}}\left[\sum_{d=1}^{2}E_{p,\tau}\left(u\,;\,Q_{r}(x_{d},t_{0})\right)\right]^{p_{\#}}

for some constant
c=c(\tau)
.

Estimate of
J
.

We first note that

\displaystyle J\leq c\sum_{d=1}^{2}\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{{r}}(t_{0})}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{B_{{r}}(x_{d})}\frac{|u-(u)_{Q_{{r}}(x_{d},t_{0})}|^{2}}{{r}^{2s+2\tau}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{p_{\#}}{2}}+\frac{|(u)_{Q_{{r}}(x_{1},t_{0})}-(u)_{Q_{{r}}(x_{2},t_{0})}|^{p_{\#}}}{{r}^{p_{\#}(s+\tau)}},

where we denote the last term by
\hat{J}
.
In light of Jensen’s inequality, (
5.47
) and (
5.48
), we estimate
\hat{J}
as

\displaystyle\hat{J}\leq\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{Q_{r}(x_{1},t_{0})}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{B_{r}(x_{2})}|u(x,t)-u(y,t)|^{p}\,dy\,dz\right)^{\frac{p_{\#}}{p}}\leq\frac{c\dist\left(B_{r}(x_{1}),B_{r}(x_{2})\right)^{p_{\#}(s+\tau)}}{{r}^{p_{\#}(s+\tau)}}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.4965pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.23955pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.45624pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-1.6875pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{p_{\#}}{p}}.

We finally combine all the estimates
I_{1}
,
I_{2}
and
J
to get the desired result (
5.49
).
∎

Remark 8
.

Let
\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\in\mathcal{A}
. By (
5.42
) and (
5.45
), we deduce
\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})
satisfies (
5.46
).
In light of (
5.37
) and Lemma
5.2
, there is a constant
c_{od}=c_{od}(n,s,p,\tau)
such that

(5.49)

\displaystyle\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p_{\#}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p_{\#}}}

\displaystyle\leq c_{od}\lambda.

Step 5. Decomposition of the family
\mathcal{A}
.

We now decompose the family
\mathcal{A}
into subfamilies
AD_{\lambda}=\bigcap\limits_{d=1}^{2}AD_{\lambda}^{d}
and
ND_{\lambda}=\bigcup\limits_{d=1}^{2}ND_{\lambda}^{d}
for

(5.50)

AD_{\lambda}^{d}=\left\{\mathcal{Q}=B^{1}\times B^{2}\times I\in\mathcal{A}\,:\quad E_{p,\tau}\left(u\,;\,{B}^{d}\times I\right)\leq\frac{\lambda}{16}\right\}

and

ND_{\lambda}^{d}=\left\{\mathcal{Q}=B^{1}\times B^{2}\times I\in\mathcal{A}\,:\quad E_{p,\tau}\left(u\,;\,{B}^{d}\times I\right)>\frac{\lambda}{16}\right\}.

Recalling (
5.36
), (
5.32
) and (
5.20
), we observe that for any
\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\in ND_{\lambda}^{d}
, there is an exit-radius
\rho_{(x_{d,j},t_{0,j})}\geq\overline{r}_{j}
such that (
5.20
) holds with
z_{0}=(x_{d,j},t_{0,j})
.
Thus, there is a cylinder
{Q}_{\rho_{i}}(z_{i})
which is selected in (
5.21
) such that

(5.51)

{Q}_{2^{j_{0}}\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{d,j},t_{0,j})={Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{d,j},t_{0,j})\subset{Q}_{2^{j_{0}}\rho_{(x_{d},t_{0})}}(x_{d,j},t_{0,j})\subset{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{i}}(z_{i})\quad\text{and}\quad\frac{\rho_{i}}{2}\leq 2^{j_{0}}\rho_{(x_{d},t_{0})}\leq 2\rho_{i}.

We have used (
5.22
) to obtain the second observation in (
5.51
). Therefore, the set

(5.52)

\mathcal{A}^{d}_{i,j,l}\coloneqq\left\{\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\in ND_{\lambda}^{d}\ :\begin{array}[]{l}{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}({x}_{d,j},t_{0,j})\subset{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{i}}(z_{i})\text{, where $i$ is the smallest integer}\\
\text{satisfying }\eqref{cz non pa : exitoffim}\text{ with }\rho_{i}\leq 2^{l}\overline{r}_{j}<2\rho_{i}\end{array}\right\}

is either a singleton or an empty set for any
l\geq 0
. Then we will verify

(5.53)

\rho_{i}<\mathrm{dist}(B_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j}),B_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{2,j})).

Suppose not, then we have

|x_{d^{\prime},j}-x_{i}|\leq|x_{d^{\prime},j}-x_{d,j}|+|x_{d,j}-x_{i}|\leq 5\rho_{i}+5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{i}<2\times 5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{i},

where
d^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}\setminus\{d\}
. Consequently, we have

\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\subset\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{2}{s}}\rho_{i}}(z_{i}),

which contradicts the definition of
\mathcal{A}
defined in (
5.45
), and the claim follows.

We next define for any
k\geq 0
,

(5.54)

\mathcal{A}^{d}_{i,j,l,k}=\left\{\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\in\mathcal{A}^{d}_{i,j,l}\,:\,2^{k}\rho_{i}\leq\mathrm{dist}(B_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j}),B_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{2,j}))<2^{k+1}\rho_{i}\right\}

to see that

(5.55)

ND^{d}_{\lambda}=\bigcup_{i}\bigcup_{j}\bigcup_{l\geq 0,k\geq 0}\mathcal{A}^{d}_{i,j,l,k}.

We first observe that if
\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\in ND_{\lambda}^{d}
, then we have

(5.56)

\displaystyle\mathfrak{A}\left(\mathcal{B}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j})\right)^{s+\tau}E_{p}(u;\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{d,j},t_{0,j})

\displaystyle\leq c{\mathfrak{A}\left(\mathcal{B}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j})\right)}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{d,j},t_{0,j})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle+\frac{c\mathfrak{A}\left(\mathcal{B}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j})\right)}{\delta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{d,j},t_{0,j})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\tilde{q}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{c\mathfrak{A}\left(\mathcal{B}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j})\right)}{\delta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{d,j},t_{0,j})}(\widetilde{r}_{j}^{s-\tau}|G|)^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}+\frac{\lambda}{100}

for some constant
c=c(n,s,L,q,\tau)
, where the constant
\widetilde{r}_{j}
is determined in (
5.39
). By following the same lines as in the proof of (
5.27
), (
5.13
) along with (
5.37
) and the fact that

\displaystyle\mathfrak{A}\left(\mathcal{B}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j})\right)\leq\mathfrak{A}\left(\mathcal{B}_{c\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j})\right)

for any
c\geq 1
,
we get

\displaystyle\mathfrak{A}\left(\mathcal{B}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j}\right)\tau^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\left(\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{r}(t_{0})}\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{B_{r}(x_{0})}\frac{|u-(u)_{Q_{r}(z_{0})}|^{2}}{r^{2s+2\tau}}\,dx\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq c{\mathfrak{A}\left(\mathcal{B}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j})\right)}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2^{j_{0}}\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{d,j},t_{0,j})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{c\mathfrak{A}\left(\mathcal{B}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j})\right)}{\delta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2^{j_{0}}\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{d,j},t_{0,j})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\tilde{q}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{c\mathfrak{A}\left(\mathcal{B}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j})\right)}{\delta}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{d,j},t_{0,j})}(r_{j}^{s-\tau}|G|)^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}+\frac{\lambda}{100}

for some constant
c=c(n,s,L,q,\tau)
. Thus, using (
5.32
), (
5.13
) and (
5.39
), we get the desired estimate.

We end this step with the following lemma which is an essential ingredient for the next step.

Lemma 5.3
.

Let us fix
i,l,k\geq 0
and
d\in\{1,2\}
. Then there is a constant
c=c(n,s,L,q,\tau)
such that

(5.57)

\sum_{\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\in\bigcup\limits_{\tilde{j}\geq 0}\mathcal{A}^{1}_{i,\tilde{j},l,k}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,j})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\leq c2^{n(l+k)}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{i}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t},

(5.58)

\sum_{\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\in\bigcup\limits_{\tilde{j}\geq 0}\mathcal{A}^{1}_{i,\tilde{j},l,k}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,j})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\leq c2^{n(l+k)}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{i}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}

and

(5.59)

\sum_{\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\in\bigcup\limits_{\tilde{j}\geq 0}\mathcal{A}^{1}_{i,\tilde{j},l,k}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,j})}(a\overline{r}_{j}^{s-\tau}|G|)^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\leq c2^{n(l+k)}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{i}}(z_{i})}(\rho_{i}^{s-\tau}|G|)^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}

Proof.

We first observe that if

(5.60)

\bigcap_{\begin{subarray}{c}j\in J\\
\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\in\bigcup\limits_{\tilde{j}\geq 0}\mathcal{A}^{1}_{i,\tilde{j},l,k}\end{subarray}}\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,j})\neq\emptyset

holds for some index set
J
, then
|J|\leq c2^{n(l+k)}
for some constant
c
depending only on
n
and
a
, where
|J|
denotes the number of elements in the set
J
. Next, suppose that
(x_{1},t_{0})\in\bigcap_{j\in J}{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,j})
. Then, by the definition of the set
\mathcal{A}^{1}_{i,j,l,k}
given in (
5.54
), we get

(5.61)

\mathrm{dist}(x_{1},B_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{2,j}))<2^{k+1}\rho_{i}+2\widetilde{r}_{j}<c2^{k}\rho_{i}

for some constant
c=c(n,s,L,q,\tau)
, where we have used the fact that
\overline{r}_{j}\leq 2^{1-l}\rho_{i}
by (
5.52
), (
5.39
) and (
5.13
). Since the set
\left\{\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\right\}_{j\in J}
is a mutually disjoint, we note from (
5.60
) that
\left\{B_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{2,j})\right\}_{j\in J}
is also a mutually disjoint set. This along with (
5.61
) implies

|J|\leq\frac{\left|B_{c2^{k}\rho_{i}}\right|}{\left|B_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}\right|}\leq c2^{n(k+l)}

for some constant
c=c(n,s,L,q,\tau)
, where we have used (
5.39
), (
5.13
) and the relation between
\overline{r}_{j}
and
\rho_{i}
given in (
5.52
). This proves (
5.60
).
We are now ready to prove (
5.57
) using an inductive argument. We first note that the number of elements in
\bigcup\limits_{j}\mathcal{A}^{1}_{i,j,l,k}
is finite, as each cylinder in
\bigcup\limits_{j}\mathcal{A}^{1}_{i,j,l,k}
is of the form
\mathcal{Q}=\mathcal{Q}_{r}
, where
2^{-l}\rho_{i}<r\leq 2^{-l+1}\rho_{i}
, is mutually disjoint and is contained in
\mathcal{Q}_{r_{2}}
. Let us denote
j_{i}
as the number of elements in
\bigcup\limits_{j}\mathcal{A}^{1}_{i,j,l,k}
. For a clear notation, we assume
\mathcal{A}^{1}_{i,j,l,k}\neq\emptyset
if
j\leq j_{i}
and
\mathcal{A}^{1}_{i,j,l,k}=\emptyset
if
j\geq j_{i}+1
. By (
5.51
), we note
\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,j})\subset Q_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{i}}(z_{i})
. We first define

\mathcal{D}_{1}=\left\{\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{i}}(z_{i})\cap\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,1}),\,\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{i}}(z_{i})\setminus\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,1},t_{0,1}),\right\}.

Suppose
\mathcal{D}_{k}
is determined, where
k\geq 1
, then we define

\mathcal{D}_{k+1}=\bigcup_{\mathcal{F}\in\mathcal{D}_{k}}\left\{\mathcal{F}\cap\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{k+1}}(x_{1,k+1},t_{0,k+1}),\,\mathcal{F}\setminus\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{k+1}}(x_{1,k+1},t_{0,k+1})\right\}.

In this way, we obtain a collection
\mathcal{D}_{j_{i}}
. Indeed, for any choice of two elements
\mathcal{F}
and
\mathcal{F}^{\prime}
in
\mathcal{D}_{j_{i}}
, we see that either
\mathcal{F}=\mathcal{F}^{\prime}
or
\mathcal{F}\cap\mathcal{F}^{\prime}=\emptyset
. Thus we can write

(5.62)

\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{i}}(z_{i})=\bigcup_{\mathcal{F}_{m}\in\mathcal{D}_{j_{i}}}{\mathcal{F}_{m}},

where
\mathcal{F}_{m}
’s are mutually disjoint, and for any
m
and
j\in[1,j_{i}]
, either

(5.63)

\mathcal{F}_{m}\subset\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,j})\quad\text{or}\quad\mathcal{F}_{m}\cap\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,j})=\emptyset.

Moreover, there are mutually disjoint elements
\mathcal{F}_{m}\in\mathcal{D}_{j_{i}}
such that

(5.64)

\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,j})=\bigcup_{\mathcal{F}_{m}\subset\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,j})}\mathcal{F}_{m}.

We note that for each
\mathcal{F}_{m}
, the number of elements in
J\coloneqq\{j\in[1,j_{i}]\,:\,\mathcal{F}_{m}\subset\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,j})\}
is at most
c2^{n(k+l)}
, due to (
5.60
).
As a result, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathcal{Q}_{\overline{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},x_{2,j},t_{0,j})\in\bigcup\limits_{\tilde{j}}\mathcal{A}^{1}_{i,\tilde{j},l,k}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,j})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{j_{i}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m\\
\mathcal{F}_{m}\subset\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,j})\end{subarray}}\int_{\mathcal{F}_{m}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}

\displaystyle=\sum_{m}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j=1\\
\mathcal{F}_{m}\subset\mathcal{Q}_{\widetilde{r}_{j}}(x_{1,j},t_{0,j})\end{subarray}}^{j_{i}}\int_{\mathcal{F}_{m}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}

\displaystyle\leq c2^{n(l+k)}\sum_{m}\int_{\mathcal{F}_{m}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}

\displaystyle\leq c2^{n(l+k)}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{i}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}

for some constant
c=c(n,s,L,q,\tau)
, which implies the desired result (
5.57
). By following the same lines as in the proof of (
5.57
) with
(D^{\tau}d_{s}u,p)
replaced by
(D^{\tau}d_{0}f,\tilde{q})
and
((\widetilde{r}_{j}^{s-\tau}|G|)^{\gamma},1)
, respectively and by using the fact that
\widetilde{r}_{j}\leq 2\rho_{i}
which follows from (
5.51
), we prove (
5.58
) and (
5.59
).
∎

Step 6. Measure estimate of
\mathcal{Q}\in AD_{\lambda}
.
We claim that for every
\mathcal{Q}\in AD_{\lambda}
, there holds

(5.65)

\mu_{\tau,t}(\mathcal{Q})\leq\frac{2^{q}}{\lambda^{p}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}\cap\left\{|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|>\frac{\lambda}{16}\right\}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}.

Indeed, on account of (
5.37
), we have

(5.66)

\displaystyle\lambda^{p}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{p}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)+2^{p}\left(\mathfrak{A}(\mathcal{K})\right)^{p}\left[\sum_{d=1}^{2}E_{p,\tau}\left(u\,;\,{B}^{d}\times I\right)\right]^{p}.

Using (
5.66
), (
5.50
) and the fact that

\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)\leq\frac{\lambda^{p}}{8^{p}}+\frac{1}{\mu_{\tau,t}(\mathcal{Q})}\int_{\mathcal{Q}\cap\left\{|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|>\frac{\lambda}{4}\right\}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t},

we deduce that

\frac{\lambda^{p}}{2^{p}}\leq\frac{1}{\mu_{\tau,t}(\mathcal{Q})}\int_{\mathcal{Q}\cap\left\{|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|>\frac{\lambda}{16}\right\}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}.

This proves the claim of (
5.65
) as
p\leq q
.

Step 7. Measure estimate of
\mathcal{Q}\in ND_{\lambda}
.

Our next aim is to estimate
\mu_{\tau,t}(\mathcal{Q})
for
\mathcal{Q}\in ND_{\lambda}
.

With the aid of (
5.65
), (
5.55
) and Lemma
5.3
, we prove the following result.

Lemma 5.4
.

There exists a constant
c=c(n,s,L,q,\tau)
such that

(5.67)

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathcal{Q}\in ND_{\lambda}}\mu_{\tau,t}(\mathcal{Q})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c}{\lambda^{p}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{r_{2}}\cap\{|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|>\frac{\lambda}{16}\}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}+c\sum_{i}\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{i}}(z_{i})\right).

Proof.

Let
\mathcal{Q}\equiv\mathcal{Q}_{r}(x_{1},x_{2},t_{0})\in ND_{\lambda}
.
We note from (
5.40
), (
5.50
), (
5.39
) and (
5.56
) that there is a constant
c=c(n,s,L,q,\tau)
such that

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{4}\leq\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+c\sum_{d=1}^{2}\left[\left(E^{d}_{u}(\mathcal{Q})\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\left(E^{d}_{f}(\mathcal{Q})\right)^{\frac{1}{\tilde{q}}}+\left(E^{d}_{g}(\mathcal{Q})\right)^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\right],

if
\mathcal{Q}\in ND^{1}_{\lambda}\cap ND^{2}_{\lambda}
and

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda}{4}\leq\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+c\left[\left(E^{d}_{u}(\mathcal{Q})\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\left(E^{d}_{f}(\mathcal{Q})\right)^{\frac{1}{\tilde{q}}}+\left(E^{d}_{g}(\mathcal{Q})\right)^{\frac{1}{\gamma}}\right],

if
\mathcal{Q}\in ND^{d}_{\lambda}\cap AD^{d^{\prime}}_{\lambda}
,
where we denote
d^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}\setminus\{d\}
.
where we denote

(5.68)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{d}_{u}\left(\mathcal{Q}\right)=\mathfrak{A}\left(\mathcal{B}_{r}(x_{1},x_{2})\right)^{p\left(s+\tau\right)}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2^{j_{0}}r}(x_{d},t_{0})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right),

(5.69)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{d}_{f}\left(\mathcal{Q}\right)=\mathfrak{A}\left(\mathcal{B}_{r}(x_{1},x_{2})\right)^{\tilde{q}\left(s+\tau\right)}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{2^{j_{0}}\mathcal{Q}_{r}(x_{d},t_{0})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right),

and

(5.70)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}^{d}_{g}\left(\mathcal{Q}\right)=\mathfrak{A}\left(\mathcal{B}_{r}(x_{1},x_{2})\right)^{\gamma\left(s+\tau\right)}\left(\mathchoice{{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-$}}\kern-4.86108pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-$}}\kern-3.43057pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.908pt}}{{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-$}}\kern-2.76045pt}}\!\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{2^{j_{0}}r}(x_{d},t_{0})}((2^{j_{0}}r)^{s-\tau}|G|)^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)

We note that the set function
\mathfrak{A}(\cdot)
is defined in (
5.34
). Using (
5.41
) and then multiplying
\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}\right)
along with a few simple calculations, we obtain

(5.71)

\displaystyle\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c}{\lambda^{p}}\int_{{\mathcal{Q}}\cap\left\{|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|>\frac{\lambda}{16}\right\}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}

\displaystyle+\frac{\mu_{\tau,t}(\mathcal{Q})}{\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{r}(x_{d},t_{0})\right)}\left[\frac{c}{\lambda^{p}}\sum_{d=1}^{2}\mathbb{E}^{d}_{u}\left(\mathcal{Q}\right)+\frac{c}{(\delta\lambda)^{\tilde{q}}}\sum_{d=1}^{2}\mathbb{E}^{d}_{f}\left(\mathcal{Q}\right)+\frac{c}{(\delta\lambda)^{b_{\tau}}}\sum_{d=1}^{2}\mathbb{E}^{d}_{g}\left(\mathcal{Q}\right)\right]

if
\mathcal{Q}\in ND^{1}_{\lambda}\cap ND^{2}_{\lambda}
and

(5.72)

\displaystyle\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c}{\lambda^{p}}\int_{\mathcal{Q}\cap\left\{|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|>\frac{\lambda}{16}\right\}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}

\displaystyle+\frac{\mu_{\tau,t}(\mathcal{Q})}{\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{r}(x_{d},t_{0})\right)}\left[\frac{c}{\lambda^{p}}\mathbb{E}^{d}_{u}\left(\mathcal{Q}\right)+\frac{c}{(\delta\lambda)^{\tilde{q}}}\mathbb{E}^{d}_{f}\left(\mathcal{Q}\right)+\frac{c}{(\delta\lambda)^{b_{\tau}}}\mathbb{E}^{d}_{g}\left(\mathcal{Q}\right)\right]

if
\mathcal{Q}\in ND^{d}_{\lambda}\cap AD^{d^{\prime}}_{\lambda}
. We next observe from (
2.4
) and (
5.48
) that

(5.73)

\frac{\mu_{\tau,t}(\mathcal{Q})}{\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{r}(x_{d},t_{0})\right)}\leq c\tau\left(\frac{r}{\dist(B_{r}(x_{1}),B_{r}(x_{2}))}\right)^{n-2\tau}

for some constant
c=c(n)
.
On account of (
5.52
), (
5.54
), (
5.55
), (
5.73
) and (
5.57
), we find that

(5.74)

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathcal{Q}\in ND_{\lambda}^{d}}\frac{\mu_{\tau,t}(\mathcal{Q})}{\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{r}(x_{d},t_{0})\right)}\mathbb{E}^{d}_{u}(\mathcal{Q})

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{i,j,k,l}\sum_{\mathcal{Q}\in\mathcal{A}^{d}_{i,j,l,k}}\left(\frac{r}{\dist(B_{r}(x_{1}),B_{r}(x_{2}))}\right)^{n-2\tau+ps+p\tau}\left(\int_{2^{j_{0}}P^{d}\mathcal{Q}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{i,l,k\geq 0}\left(2^{-(l+k)}\right)^{ps}\left(\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{i}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right)

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{i\geq 0}\left(\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{i}}(z_{i})}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}\right),

where we denote
P^{d}\mathcal{Q}=\mathcal{Q}_{r}(x_{d},t_{0})
for a given cylinder
\mathcal{Q}=\mathcal{Q}_{r}(x_{1},x_{2},t_{0})
. For the last inequality, we have used the fact that

\sum_{i,j\geq 0}\left(2^{-(i+j)}\right)^{a}\leq\frac{2^{a}}{a\mathrm{ln}2}\quad(a>0).

Therefore, using (
5.20
), we obtain

\sum_{\mathcal{Q}\in ND_{\lambda}^{d}}\frac{\mu_{\tau,t}(\mathcal{Q})}{\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{r}(x_{d},t_{0})\right)}\mathbb{E}^{d}_{u}(\mathcal{Q})\leq c\lambda^{p}\sum_{i}\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{i}}(z_{i})\right)

As in (
5.74
) along with (
5.58
), (
5.59
) and (
5.20
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathcal{Q}\in ND_{\lambda}^{d}}\frac{\mu_{\tau,t}(\mathcal{Q})}{\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{r}(x_{d},t_{0})\right)}\mathbb{E}^{d}_{f}(\mathcal{Q})

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{i,l,k\geq 0}\left(2^{-(l+k)}\right)^{\tilde{q}s}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{i}}(x_{d},t_{0})}|D^{\tau}d_{0}f|^{\tilde{q}}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}

\displaystyle\leq c(\delta\lambda)^{\tilde{q}}\sum_{i}\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{i}}(z_{i})\right)

and

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathcal{Q}\in ND_{\lambda}^{d}}\frac{\mu_{\tau,t}(\mathcal{Q})}{\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{r}(x_{d},t_{0})\right)}\mathbb{E}^{d}_{f}(\mathcal{Q})

\displaystyle\leq c\sum_{i,l,k\geq 0}\left(2^{-(l+k)}\right)^{\gamma s}\int_{\mathcal{Q}_{5^{\frac{1}{s}}\rho_{i}}(x_{d},t_{0})}\left((\rho_{i})^{s-\tau}|G|\right)^{\gamma}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}

\displaystyle\leq c(\delta\lambda)^{\gamma}\sum_{i}\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{i}}(z_{i})\right)

for some constant
c=c(n,s,L,q,\tau)
.
Combining the above two inequalities, we get

(5.75)

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathcal{Q}\in ND_{\lambda}^{d}}\frac{\mu_{\tau,t}(\mathcal{Q})}{\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{r}(x_{d},t_{0})\right)}\left[\frac{1}{(\delta\lambda)^{\tilde{q}}}\mathbb{E}^{d}_{f}(\mathcal{Q})+\frac{1}{(\delta\lambda)^{\gamma}}\mathbb{E}^{d}_{g}(\mathcal{Q})\right]\leq c\sum_{i}\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{i}}(z_{i})\right),

where we denote
\mathcal{Q}=B^{1}\times B^{2}\times\Lambda
.
Plugging (
5.74
) and (
5.75
) into (
5.71
) and (
5.72
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathcal{Q}\in ND_{\lambda}}\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c}{\lambda^{p}}\int_{{\mathcal{Q}_{r_{2}}}\cap\left\{|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|>\frac{\lambda}{16}\right\}}|D^{\tau}d_{s}u|^{p}\,d\mu_{\tau,t}+c\sum_{i}\mu_{\tau,t}\left(\mathcal{Q}_{\rho_{i}}(z_{i})\right)

∎

Step 8. Completion of the proof.

Considering (
5.18
) and (
5.36
), the constant
\frac{M}{\kappa}
depends only on
n,s,L,q
and
\tau
.
Therefore, if
\lambda\geq\lambda_{0}
which is determined in (
5.12
), then we find two families of countable disjoint cylinders
